        static void Stretch(RectTransform rt)
        {
            if (rt == null) return;
            rt.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
            rt.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            rt.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;

   530|
        void CachePanelSprite()
        {
            var images = GetComponentsInChildren<Image>(true);
            for (var i = 0; i < images.Length; i++)
            {
                var sprite = images[i] != null ? images[i].sprite : null;
                if (sprite == null) continue;
                _panelSprite = sprite;
                _panelSliced = sprite.border.sqrMagnitude > 0.01f;
   540|                return;
            }
            _panelSprite = WhiteSprite();
            _panelSliced = false;
        }

        static void ApplyPanelSprite(Image image)

   550|            image.type = _panelSliced ? Image.Type.Sliced : Image.Type.Simple;

        }

        static TMP_FontAsset ResolveFont()
        {

            }
            return TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset;
        }
    }
}

        void RemoveLabel(string name)
        {

        void EnsureCard()
        {
            var existing = transform.Find("MenuCard");
            RectTransform rt;
            Image image;
            if (existing == null)
            {
                var go = new GameObject("MenuCard", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image));
                go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
                go.transform.SetSiblingIndex(1);
                rt = go.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
                image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
            }
            else
            {
                rt = existing.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
                image = existing.GetComponent<Image>();
            }
            rt.anchorMin = rt.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.anchoredPosition = Vector2.zero;
            rt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(620f, 440f);
            if (image == null) return;
            image.color = Paper;
            image.raycastTarget = false;
            if (image.sprite == null)
                ApplyPanelSprite(image);
        }

        Button LayoutButton(string name, string key, float y, TMP_FontAsset font)
        {
            var go = GameObject.Find(name);
            if (go == null) return null;
            var button = go.GetComponent<Button>();
            var rt = go.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            if (rt != null)
            {
                rt.anchorMin = rt.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
                rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
                rt.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(0f, y);
                rt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(ButtonWidth, ButtonHeight);
            }
            if (button != null)
            {
                var colors = button.colors;
                colors.normalColor = ButtonNormal;
                colors.highlightedColor = ButtonHighlight;
                colors.pressedColor = ButtonPressed;
                colors.selectedColor = ButtonHighlight;
                colors.disabledColor = new Color(ButtonNormal.r, ButtonNormal.g, ButtonNormal.b, 0.35f);
                colors.fadeDuration = 0.08f;
                button.colors = colors;
            }
            var label = go.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>();
            if (label != null)
            {
                label.fontSize = 30f;
                label.color = Cream;
                label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
                label.raycastTarget = false;
                if (font != null) label.font = font;
                Bind(label, key);
            }
            return button;
        }

### Turn 71 (Assistant)

主菜单场景里你摆好的物体，运行时不会再被改位置，也不会被打开、关闭或删掉。

### 数据流

```
SetGlobalVar（对话 / ClueTrigger / 老鼠商店）
  → BookController_OnVarChanged
  → CheckAllEntries（条件满足则渐显 entry_*）
  → ReconcileLinksAndMods（link_* / mod_*）
   330|```

- 条件语法：`&` AND、`|` OR；`VarName==true`、`VarName>=2` 等
- 入册演出：解锁后保持 alpha=0；打开本子 / 翻页到该页时（`ShowCurrentPage`）播 1s 渐显 + 一次 alpha 闪烁
- `E10` 入册：自动 `currentIndex = 2`（翻到第二页）

完整 Inspector 字段名对照表见 [`09` §9.8](../MissingEggDoc-main/docs/09-侦探笔记本.md#98-inspector-接线对照bookcontroller)（与 `BookController.lua` `---@var` 一一对应）。

## 8. 典型第一章链路（代码视角）

   360|1. 点 **淑芬** → `shufang_01` 分支 1 → `Shufen_CommissionDone=true`，UnlockBranches → 淑芬分支 2
2. 调查 **E01/E02/E03** → ClueTrigger 写入 → 间接推高 `ChickTraceCount`
3. 点 **小鸡侦探团** → `xiaojiZTT_02` hub → `ChickTraceCount>=2` 时显示质询选项 → `ChickStatus=2`
4. 点 **大黄** → `dahuang_01` → `DogStatus=2`
5. **E06** 发现缺梯 → `E06_ViewNeedLadder=true`
6. 大黄分支 2/3 → 借梯/醒酒 → `E06_LadderBorrowed=true` → `DaHuang.lua` 切换模型
7. 谷仓顶 **E10** 等 → 对应 `entry_*` 渐显；E10 自动翻到第二页
8. 进入第二章 → 红顶屋外 E13~E18 → 黑猫线 → 薄荷鱼线 → （漫画收束待实现）

详细步骤见 [`07-关卡与内容结构`](../MissingEggDoc-main/docs/07-关卡与内容结构.md)。

   400|## 10. 开发备忘

- PC：`GameBootstrap` Play 后自动生成玩家胶囊与第三人称相机；菜单 `Tools/Egg Rescue/PC/Setup Scene For PC Runtime` 写入 `PlayerSpawn`、按 lua 名迁移 C# 组件并禁用 `DouyinScript`。
- 操作：WASD 移动、空格跳、鼠标视角、只有 E 键开始交互；对话中鼠标左键 / 空格 / 回车下一句；N 开关笔记本；F5 存档、F9 读档、Esc 解锁鼠标。
- 存档：`Application.persistentDataPath/windmill_farm_save.json`（变量 + NPC 分支 + 奶酪拾取 + BranchFlag + 已发现交互点）。主菜单「继续游戏」读档后进入；「开始游戏」从默认状态进入，已有存档保留到游戏中再次 F5。编辑器直接 Play `Mechanics_Code` **不**自动读档。
- 对话 JSON 漏字段会卡主线：`ShopAction`、`RotatePool`、`ChainDialogue`、`BranchFlag`、`DisplayConditions`。
- Scene 物体名 `MianController` 为历史拼写。Lua `DialogueTrigger` 仍通过 `GameObject.Find("DialogueData")` 读 DouyinScript；C# 改为 `Resources/GameData/Dialogue`。
- 不要移植 `ClueTrigger.Awake` 的全量 bool 重置。

        public static void SetPaused(bool paused)
        {
            Paused = paused;
            if (paused)
            {
                Cursor.lockState = CursorLockMode.None;
                Cursor.visible = true;
                return;
            }
            if (!DialogueActive && !NotebookOpen)
            {
                Cursor.lockState = CursorLockMode.Locked;
                Cursor.visible = false;
            }
        }

        public static void ResetSession()
        {
            DialogueActive = false;
            NotebookOpen = false;
            Paused = false;
        }

        static void ApplySavedPose(GameObject player)
        {
            if (!SaveService.HasPose || player == null) return;
            var controller = player.GetComponent<PlayerController>();
            if (controller != null)
                controller.Teleport(SaveService.PosePosition, SaveService.PoseYaw);
            else
                player.transform.SetPositionAndRotation(SaveService.PosePosition, Quaternion.Euler(0f, SaveService.PoseYaw, 0f));
            if (ThirdPersonCamera.Instance != null)
                ThirdPersonCamera.Instance.SetView(SaveService.PoseYaw, SaveService.PosePitch);
        }

        static void ReadPose(JsonValue player)
        {
            HasPose = false;
            if (player.Type != JsonValue.Kind.Object) return;
            if (player["x"].Type != JsonValue.Kind.Number) return;
            PosePosition = new Vector3(Num(player["x"]), Num(player["y"]), Num(player["z"]));
            PoseYaw = Num(player["yaw"]);
            PosePitch = Num(player["pitch"]);
            HasPose = true;
        }

        static float Num(JsonValue value)
        {
            if (value.Type != JsonValue.Kind.Number) return 0f;
            return (float)value.NumberValue;
        }

        {

### Turn 167 (Assistant)

[tool_request: Write]
- path: <USER_PROFILE>\Documents\GitHub\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\Assets\Scripts\Runtime\UI\PauseMenu.cs
- contents: using System.Collections.Generic;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.SceneManagement;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace EggRescue
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 游戏中按 Esc 暂停。保存后回到主菜单，继续游戏会读这份存档。
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class PauseMenu : MonoBehaviour
    {
        static readonly Color Paper = new Color(0.95f, 0.91f, 0.82f, 1f);
        static readonly Color Ink = new Color(0.14f, 0.23f, 0.16f, 1f);
        static readonly Color InkSoft = new Color(0.35f, 0.40f, 0.31f, 1f);
        static readonly Color Cream = new Color(0.965f, 0.945f, 0.894f, 1f);
        static readonly Color ButtonNormal = new Color(0.18f, 0.33f, 0.22f, 1f);
        static readonly Color ButtonHighlight = new Color(0.26f, 0.45f, 0.30f, 1f);
        static readonly Color ButtonPressed = new Color(0.12f, 0.22f, 0.15f, 1f);

        static Sprite _white;

        GameObject _root;
        TMP_Text _status;
        string _statusKey;
        readonly List<BoundLabel> _labels = new List<BoundLabel>();

        sealed class BoundLabel
        {
            public TMP_Text Label;
            public string Key;
        }

        void Awake()
        {
            Build(ResolveFont());
            SetOpen(false);
        }

        void OnEnable()
        {
            GameLocale.Changed += RefreshLocale;
        }

        void OnDisable()
        {
            GameLocale.Changed -= RefreshLocale;
        }

        void Update()
        {
            if (!Input.GetKeyDown(KeyCode.Escape)) return;
            if (_root == null) return;
            SetOpen(!_root.activeSelf);
        }

        void SetOpen(bool open)
        {
            if (_root != null) _root.SetActive(open);
            GameEvents.SetPaused(open);
            Time.timeScale = open ? 0f : 1f;
            AudioListener.pause = open;
            if (open) SetStatus(null);
        }

        void SaveProgress()
        {
            SetStatus(SaveService.Save() ? "pause.saved" : "pause.save_failed");
        }

        void ReturnToMenu()
        {
            Time.timeScale = 1f;
            AudioListener.pause = false;
            GameEvents.ResetSession();
            Cursor.lockState = CursorLockMode.None;
            Cursor.visible = true;
            GameSession.Pending = GameSession.Mode.Default;
            SceneManager.LoadScene(GameSession.MenuSceneName);
            if (GameBootstrap.Instance != null)
                Destroy(GameBootstrap.Instance.gameObject);
        }

        void Build(TMP_FontAsset font)
        {
            EnsureEventSystem();
            var canvasGo = new GameObject("PauseCanvas", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(Canvas), typeof(CanvasScaler), typeof(GraphicRaycaster));
            canvasGo.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
            var canvas = canvasGo.GetComponent<Canvas>();
            canvas.renderMode = RenderMode.ScreenSpaceOverlay;
            canvas.sortingOrder = 300;
            var scaler = canvasGo.GetComponent<CanvasScaler>();
            scaler.uiScaleMode = CanvasScaler.ScaleMode.ScaleWithScreenSize;
            scaler.referenceResolution = new Vector2(1920f, 1080f);
            scaler.matchWidthOrHeight = 0.5f;

            _root = new GameObject("PauseRoot", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image));
            _root.transform.SetParent(canvasGo.transform, false);
            Stretch(_root.GetComponent<RectTransform>());
            var dim = _root.GetComponent<Image>();
            dim.color = new Color(0.05f, 0.08f, 0.06f, 0.62f);
            dim.sprite = WhiteSprite();
            dim.raycastTarget = true;

            var card = new GameObject("Paper", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image));
            card.transform.SetParent(_root.transform, false);
            var cardRt = card.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            cardRt.anchorMin = cardRt.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            cardRt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            cardRt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(560f, 520f);
            var cardImage = card.GetComponent<Image>();
            cardImage.color = Paper;
            cardImage.sprite = WhiteSprite();
            cardImage.raycastTarget = true;

            AddLabel(cardRt, "pause.title", new Vector2(0f, 180f), new Vector2(480f, 64f), 42f, Ink, font);
            CreateButton(cardRt, "pause.resume", new Vector2(0f, 70f), font, () => SetOpen(false));
            CreateButton(cardRt, "pause.save", new Vector2(0f, -18f), font, SaveProgress);
            CreateButton(cardRt, "pause.main_menu", new Vector2(0f, -106f), font, ReturnToMenu);
            _status = AddLabel(cardRt, null, new Vector2(0f, -190f), new Vector2(480f, 40f), 22f, InkSoft, font);
        }

        void EnsureEventSystem()
        {
            if (EventSystem.current != null) return;
            var go = new GameObject("EventSystem", typeof(EventSystem), typeof(StandaloneInputModule));
            go.transform.SetParent(transform, false);
        }

        TMP_Text AddLabel(RectTransform parent, string key, Vector2 pos, Vector2 size, float fontSize, Color color, TMP_FontAsset font)
        {
            var go = new GameObject("Label", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(TextMeshProUGUI));
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            var rt = go.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            rt.anchorMin = rt.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.anchoredPosition = pos;
            rt.sizeDelta = size;
            var label = go.GetComponent<TextMeshProUGUI>();
            label.fontSize = fontSize;
            label.color = color;
            label.alignment = TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
            label.raycastTarget = false;
            label.enableWordWrapping = true;
            if (font != null) label.font = font;
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
            {
                label.text = GameLocale.T(key);
                _labels.Add(new BoundLabel { Label = label, Key = key });
            }
            return label;
        }

        void CreateButton(RectTransform parent, string key, Vector2 pos, TMP_FontAsset font, UnityEngine.Events.UnityAction onClick)
        {
            var go = new GameObject(key, typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image), typeof(Button));
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            var rt = go.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            rt.anchorMin = rt.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.anchoredPosition = pos;
            rt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(400f, 64f);
            var image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
            image.color = Color.white;
            image.sprite = WhiteSprite();
            var button = go.GetComponent<Button>();
            var colors = button.colors;
            colors.normalColor = ButtonNormal;
            colors.highlightedColor = ButtonHighlight;
            colors.pressedColor = ButtonPressed;
            colors.selectedColor = ButtonHighlight;
            colors.fadeDuration = 0.08f;
            button.colors = colors;
            button.targetGraphic = image;
            button.onClick.AddListener(onClick);
            var label = AddLabel(rt, key, Vector2.zero, new Vector2(400f, 64f), 28f, Cream, font);
            Stretch(label.rectTransform);
        }

        void SetStatus(string key)
        {
            _statusKey = key;
            if (_status == null) return;
            _status.text = string.IsNullOrEmpty(key) ? "" : GameLocale.T(key);
        }

        void RefreshLocale()
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < _labels.Count; i++)
            {
                var bound = _labels[i];
                if (bound.Label != null) bound.Label.text = GameLocale.T(bound.Key);
            }
            SetStatus(_statusKey);
        }

        static void Stretch(RectTransform rt)
        {
            if (rt == null) return;
            rt.anchorMin = Vector2.zero;
            rt.anchorMax = Vector2.one;
            rt.offsetMin = Vector2.zero;
            rt.offsetMax = Vector2.zero;
            rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
        }

        static Sprite WhiteSprite()
        {
            if (_white != null) return _white;
            var tex = new Texture2D(1, 1, TextureFormat.RGBA32, false);
            tex.SetPixel(0, 0, Color.white);
            tex.Apply();
            tex.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
            _white = Sprite.Create(tex, new Rect(0f, 0f, 1f, 1f), new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f), 1f);
            _white.hideFlags = HideFlags.HideAndDontSave;
            return _white;
        }

        static TMP_FontAsset ResolveFont()
        {
            var texts = FindObjectsOfType<TMP_Text>(true);
            for (var i = 0; i < texts.Length; i++)
            {
                if (texts[i] != null && texts[i].font != null)
                    return texts[i].font;
            }
            return TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset;
        }
    }

        {

            if (font == null) font = TryBuiltin("Arial.ttf");
            if (font == null)
            {
                var candidates = new[] { "Microsoft YaHei", "SimHei", "SimSun", "PingFang SC", "Arial" };
                for (var i = 0; i < candidates.Length && font == null; i++)
                    font = Font.CreateDynamicFontFromOSFont(candidates[i], 32);
            }
            return font;
        }

        static Font TryBuiltin(string path)
        {
            try
            {
                return Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>(path);

            catch (System.Exception)
            {
                return null;
            }

### Turn 217 (Assistant)

游戏中按 **Esc** 会打开暂停面板。面板上有三个按钮：返回游戏、保存进度、返回主菜单。再按一次 Esc，或点「返回游戏」，都会回到游戏。

**保存进度**会把当前调查进度和你所在的位置写进存档。回到主菜单后点**继续游戏**，会读这份存档：线索、角色分支、奶酪、已发现的交互点，以及保存时的位置和朝向都会恢复。没点保存就回主菜单，存档不会更新。

暂停时角色不能移动，对话和笔记本也不会继续操作。原来的 F5 存档仍然可用。

        {
            if (image == null) return;
            image.sprite = _panelSprite != null ? _panelSprite : WhiteSprite();

        }

        static void ApplySolid(Image image)
        {
            if (image == null) return;
            image.sprite = WhiteSprite();
            image.type = Image.Type.Simple;
        }

  24:| 策划 | 树状 md + `17` 变量 + **§路由规则** | 策划 |
  309:**菜单显示上限（运行时）**：条件过滤后若可显示选项 **> 4**，UI 只保留 **前 3 项 + 最后一项**。最后一项约定为告辞/结束对话（`Next = -1`），策划写菜单时须把告辞排在【菜单】块末尾；其余话题下次再点 hub 会出现。

  459:### 9.2 程序侧

docs/风车农场悬案-UNITY挑战赛作品说明文档.md
  47:- **编辑器生产管线。** 项目内有对话图编辑器、NPC 与变量编辑器，以及 Lua 发布、变量校验、Lua 转 JSON 的菜单工具，保证策划文档、对话数据和运行时保持同一份事实。

  74:| Cursor | 程序开发 | 运行时脚本、交互逻辑、编辑器工具和数据转换代码的草稿与修改建议 | 对照策划文档核对变量、分支和场景接线，由开发者修改、联调并定稿 |
  75:| GPT | 角色设计 | 角色性格、外形要点、关系位置和语气方向的文字草案 | 写入角色文档前由策划删改，统一时间线、说话边界和终局信息差 |
  81:**Cursor 负责写代码，把策划案更快落到可运行的系统上。** 本作的难点在于对话分支、物证锁、笔记本入册和场景状态必须使用同一套变量。Cursor 参与编写 C# 运行时、交互控制器、笔记本逻辑和 Lua 到 JSON 的数据管线，减少了重复样板代码的时间，让开发能把精力放在分支是否串线、线索是否漏记、存档是否完整这些品质问题上。生成结果会对照变量表和场景逐项核对，通过联调后才保留。
  83:**GPT 负责生成角色设计，让群像在动笔之前就有清晰的性格差异。** 农场里有八个主要动物角色，加上缺席的农场主。GPT 用于整理每个角色的外形、语气和戏剧功能，例如淑芬的劳模母亲、大黄的老实失职、悲伤蛙的丧气误读、老鼠兄弟的情报商人。这些文字只作为人设草案。策划会按全案时间线删掉抢戏、泄底和人设漂移的内容，再定稿为角色文档和对话脚本。
  85:**Tripo 和混元负责生成模型，用来快速试剪影和体块。** 两套工具分别产出角色和道具的过程模型，方便在 Lowpoly 农场里比较比例、轮廓和细节密度。生成网格不直接作为最终资产。入场景前会做清理、减面、修型，并按剧情需要的静态姿势重新调整，使模型符合「能演戏的动物」这一美术方向，而不是停留在生成器的默认可爱比例上。
  91:- 代码以策划文档中的变量名、分支条件和场景挂点为准，生成代码经过阅读、修改和实机验证。

docs/DIALOGUE_PIPELINE.md
  3:> **用途**：从策划树状 md → 生成/编辑 lua → 发布到运行时 Data 的标准流程。

# 风车农场悬案 UNITY挑战赛 作品说明文档

## 一、项目简介

### 1.1 游戏简介

《风车农场悬案》是一款 3D Lowpoly 轻推理游戏。玩家扮演一只绿头鸭，走进一座色彩明快、像盒子盆景一样的微缩农场，调查劳模母鸡淑芬丢失的、画有爱心标记的宝贝蛋。

农场里的动物各说各话：小鸡侦探团在撒谎，大黄宿醉后认错了嫌疑人，悲伤蛙把寻常夜景读成惊悚预言，乌鸦把假蛋当成宝贝囤在屋顶。玩家要在这些「跨频道聊天」里收集物证、核对矛盾，把一场看似惊悚的失踪案拼回它真正的样子。全流程约一小时，前段是荒诞误导，终局收成一次温柔的情感反转。
    10|
作品使用团结引擎 1.10.1 开发，目标发布平台为 Steam，支持设备为 PC。可运行场景为 `Assets/Scenes/Mechanics_Code.unity`，操作方式为第三人称行走、跳跃与键鼠交互。

初版曾上线抖音小火人世界，约五万人游玩，并获得大量好评。初版使用 Lua 编写逻辑，并用 Lua 存储数据。当前版本把运行逻辑改为 C#，把运行时数据改为 JSON。

### 1.2 核心玩法

本作是一款由轻度平台跳跃支撑的探索推理游戏。点击对话承担推理，跳跃和走位承担探索与收集，二者一起撑起「在农场里办案」。

主循环是：
    20|
1. 在农场中行走、跳跃，接近角色与环境。
2. 按交互键触发气泡对话，或调查木炭、羽毛、脚印、白石头等物证。
3. 侦探笔记本按当前进度自动写入线索、假设和下一步去向。
4. 假设被推翻后，笔记本当场划掉旧结论并补上新结论。
5. 向角色出示特定物证，解锁更深的对话。
6. 拾取散落的奶酪碎，向墙缝里的老鼠兄弟兑换情报。

单局按一条主案件推进：

    30|| 阶段 | 玩家感受 | 玩法重心 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 起因 | 焦急，带一点轻喜剧 | 接受淑芬委托，打开笔记本，开始走访 |
| 第一章：天降大锅 | 从寻凶滑向爆笑乌龙 | 池塘误导、谷仓屋顶对峙、假蛋回收 |
| 第二章：温柔的反转 | 各说各话逐渐对上 | 黑猫线、老鼠情报、青蛙与薄荷鱼、红顶屋外勘察 |
| 漫画收束 | 释然 | 跳入红顶屋二层窗后，手帐展开为漫画，完成真相与团聚 |
| 二周目：释怀盘 | 吐槽与补完 | 全角色结尾对话、老鼠八卦与奶酪碎再收集 |

侦探笔记本是线索本，也是任务指引。主线占两页手帐，老鼠情报单独成页，用便签、大头针、红线和涂改痕把推理过程留在纸面上。玩家不需要另开任务栏。

    40|### 1.3 技术亮点

- **从 Lua 迁到 C# 与 JSON。** 初版用 Lua 编写逻辑并存储数据。当前运行逻辑为 C#，运行时数据为 JSON。对话定稿仍可由 Lua 表转换成 JSON，条件分支、选项锁、物证质询和分支解锁都挂在同一套变量上，改台词不必改交互代码。
- **完整的探案状态机。** 全局变量统一登记线索、章节进度和角色状态。调查点写入变量，对话节点读写变量，笔记本监听变量变化后自动入册、连线和划掉错误假设。
- **可玩的 PC 运行时架构。** `GameBootstrap` 在进入场景后拉起玩家、相机、对话、音频、经济和结局系统。当前玩法逻辑写在 `Assets/Scripts/Runtime/`，覆盖移动、交互、对话、笔记本、奶酪经济和漫画收束。
- **手帐式笔记本。** 主线条目、老鼠情报、连线和涂改都由游戏状态驱动。新线索在翻到对应页时渐显写入，错误推论保留划痕，推理过程本身成为界面。
- **三层表现。** 3D Lowpoly 场景负责空间和角色存在感，2D 立绘负责对话情绪，终局卧室揭晓由手帐漫画分镜完成，卧室内部不在 3D 场景里实时演出。
- **编辑器生产管线。** 项目内有对话图编辑器、NPC 与变量编辑器，以及 Lua 发布、变量校验、Lua 转 JSON 的菜单工具，保证策划文档、对话数据和运行时保持同一份事实。

### 1.4 开发进度
    50|
策划案、角色关系、两章流程、对话树和全局变量表已经定稿，并落到可运行的团结引擎工程中。当前可在 PC 上完成从接委托到漫画收束的主线体验，并按 Steam 发布来准备。

| 模块 | 状态 |
| --- | --- |
| 第三人称移动、跳跃、键鼠交互 | 已可玩 |
| 对话框架（分支、条件、物证质询、变量写入） | 已实现 |
| 淑芬、小鸡侦探团、大黄、悲伤蛙、乌鸦、黑猫、老鼠兄弟等主要对话 | 已接入 |
| 环境调查点与线索变量 | 已挂入场景 |
| 侦探笔记本（主线条目、连线、划掉、老鼠情报页） | 已实现 |
    60|| 奶酪碎收集与老鼠兄弟情报商店 | 已实现 |
| 漫画收束（跳入二层窗后的结局演出） | 已实现 |
| JSON 存档（变量、角色分支、奶酪拾取、已发现交互点） | 已实现 |
| 二周目释怀盘 | 状态变量已定义，部分角色的结尾对话仍在补全 |
| 平台跳跃与空间闸门 | 场景几何已搭建，进度控制主要仍由调查点变量完成 |

## 二、AI 工具说明

本作品在开发中使用了 AI 工具，用于加快代码实现、角色构思和模型起稿。AI 产出的都是过程文件。进入工程的代码、人设、台词和模型，都经过人工审阅、修改和打磨后才定稿。

    70|### 2.1 使用的工具与应用环节

| AI 工具 | 应用环节 | 过程稿 | 人工定稿 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Cursor | 程序开发 | 运行时脚本、交互逻辑、编辑器工具和数据转换代码的草稿与修改建议 | 对照策划文档核对变量、分支和场景接线，由开发者修改、联调并定稿 |
| GPT | 角色设计 | 角色性格、外形要点、关系位置和语气方向的文字草案 | 写入角色文档前由策划删改，统一时间线、说话边界和终局信息差 |
| Tripo | 三维模型起稿 | 角色或道具的生成网格、初版体块和姿态参考 | 检查比例、破面和风格后重拓扑、清理、绑到场景并手工调整 |
| 混元 | 三维模型起稿 | 另一路生成网格，用于比较体块、剪影和细节密度 | 与 Tripo 稿一样只作起稿，入场景前完成清理、减面和手工修型 |

- path: <USER_PROFILE>\Documents\GitHub\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\Assets\Localization\Tables\UI_zh-hans.asset

[tool_request: Write]
- path: <USER_PROFILE>\Documents\GitHub\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\Assets\Scripts\Runtime\UI\MenuButtonMotion.cs
- contents: using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace EggRescue
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 主菜单按钮：悬停放大并轻轻晃动，按下后播完动画再执行动作。
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class MenuButtonMotion : MonoBehaviour, IPointerEnterHandler, IPointerExitHandler
    {
        const float HoverScale = 1.08f;
        const float ClickDuration = 0.36f;

        Button _button;
        RectTransform _rt;
        Vector3 _baseScale;
        Vector2 _basePos;
        float _hover;
        float _shake;
        bool _over;
        bool _busy;

        void Awake()
        {
            _button = GetComponent<Button>();
            _rt = transform as RectTransform;
            _baseScale = transform.localScale;
            if (_rt != null) _basePos = _rt.anchoredPosition;
        }

        void OnDisable()
        {
            RestoreRest();
            _busy = false;
            _over = false;
            _hover = 0f;
        }

        public void Play(UnityAction then)
        {
            if (_busy) return;
            if (_button != null && !_button.interactable) return;
            StartCoroutine(ClickRoutine(then));
        }

        public void OnPointerEnter(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            if (_button != null && !_button.interactable) return;
            _over = true;
        }

        public void OnPointerExit(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            _over = false;
        }

        void Update()
        {
            if (_busy || _rt == null) return;
            var target = _over && (_button == null || _button.interactable) ? 1f : 0f;
            _hover = Mathf.MoveTowards(_hover, target, Time.unscaledDeltaTime * 7f);
            if (_hover > 0.001f) _shake += Time.unscaledDeltaTime * 22f;
            else _shake = 0f;
            var blend = Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, _hover);
            var wobble = Mathf.Sin(_shake) * 3.2f * blend;
            var jitter = new Vector2(Mathf.Sin(_shake * 1.35f), Mathf.Cos(_shake * 1.7f)) * (4f * blend);
            transform.localScale = _baseScale * Mathf.Lerp(1f, HoverScale, blend);
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, wobble);
            _rt.anchoredPosition = _basePos + jitter;
        }

        IEnumerator ClickRoutine(UnityAction then)
        {
            _busy = true;
            _over = false;
            var elapsed = 0f;
            while (elapsed < ClickDuration)
            {
                elapsed += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                var p = Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / ClickDuration);
                float scale;
                if (p < 0.28f) scale = Mathf.Lerp(HoverScale, 0.88f, p / 0.28f);
                else if (p < 0.62f) scale = Mathf.Lerp(0.88f, 1.14f, (p - 0.28f) / 0.34f);
                else scale = Mathf.Lerp(1.14f, 1f, (p - 0.62f) / 0.38f);
                var wobble = Mathf.Sin(p * Mathf.PI * 5f) * (1f - p) * 7f;
                transform.localScale = _baseScale * scale;
                transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, wobble);
                if (_rt != null) _rt.anchoredPosition = _basePos;
                yield return null;
            }
            RestoreRest();
            _hover = 0f;
            _shake = 0f;
            _busy = false;
            if (then != null) then();
        }

        void RestoreRest()
        {
            transform.localScale = _baseScale;
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            if (_rt != null) _rt.anchoredPosition = _basePos;
        }
    }
}

        }

        void Wire()
        {
            Press(_start, OnStart);
            Press(_load, OnContinue);
            Press(_settings, OpenSettings);
            Press(_credits, OpenCredits);
            Press(_quit, QuitGame);
        }

        void Press(Button button, UnityEngine.Events.UnityAction action)
        {
            if (button == null || action == null) return;
            var motion = button.GetComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
            if (motion == null) motion = button.gameObject.AddComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
            button.onClick.AddListener(() =>
            {
                if (_pressLock || _entering) return;
                _pressLock = true;
                motion.Play(() =>
                {
                    _pressLock = false;
                    action();
                });
            });
        }

- new_string:         static RectTransform AddPlate(RectTransform parent, Vector2 pos, Vector2 size)
        {
            var go = new GameObject("Row", typeof(RectTransform), typeof(CanvasRenderer), typeof(Image));
            go.transform.SetParent(parent, false);
            var rt = go.GetComponent<RectTransform>();
            rt.anchorMin = rt.anchorMax = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, 0.5f);
            rt.anchoredPosition = pos;
            rt.sizeDelta = size;
            var image = go.GetComponent<Image>();
            image.color = new Color(0.90f, 0.85f, 0.74f, 1f);
            image.raycastTarget = false;
            ApplySolid(image);
            return rt;
        }

[tool_request: Write]
- path: <USER_PROFILE>\Documents\GitHub\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\Assets\Scripts\Runtime\UI\UiFontCatalog.cs
- contents: using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;

namespace EggRescue
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 界面中文用的 MiSans。图集满了之后可以继续加页，避免新字变成方框。
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class UiFontCatalog : ScriptableObject
    {
        public TMP_FontAsset Font;

        public static TMP_FontAsset Load()
        {
            var catalog = Resources.Load<UiFontCatalog>("UiFont");
            if (catalog != null && catalog.Font != null) return catalog.Font;
            var texts = FindObjectsOfType<TMP_Text>(true);
            for (var i = 0; i < texts.Length; i++)
            {
                var font = texts[i] != null ? texts[i].font : null;
                if (font != null && font.name.IndexOf("MiSans", System.StringComparison.Ordinal) >= 0)
                    return font;
            }
            for (var i = 0; i < texts.Length; i++)
            {
                if (texts[i] != null && texts[i].font != null)
                    return texts[i].font;
            }
            return TMP_Settings.defaultFontAsset;
        }
    }
}

- old_string:         static Font ResolveFont()
        {
            var candidates = new[] { "Microsoft YaHei", "SimHei", "SimSun", "PingFang SC" };
            for (var i = 0; i < candidates.Length; i++)
            {
                var font = Font.CreateDynamicFontFromOSFont(candidates[i], 32);
                if (font != null) return font;
            }
            var builtin = TryBuiltin("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
            if (builtin == null) builtin = TryBuiltin("Arial.ttf");
            return builtin;
        }

        static Font TryBuiltin(string path)
        {
            try
            {
                return Resources.GetBuiltinResource<Font>(path);
            }
            catch (System.Exception)
            {
                return null;
            }
        }
- new_string:         static TMP_FontAsset ResolveFont()
        {

        static Font ResolveUiFont()
        {
            var font = TryGetBuiltinFont("LegacyRuntime.ttf");
            if (font == null) font = TryGetBuiltinFont("Arial.ttf");
            if (font == null)
            {
                // 中文提示需要能显示汉字的字体，优先挑常见的中文系统字体。
                var candidates = new[] { "Microsoft YaHei", "SimHei", "SimSun", "PingFang SC", "Arial" };
                for (var i = 0; i < candidates.Length && font == null; i++)
                    font = Font.CreateDynamicFontFromOSFont(candidates[i], 28);
            }
            return font;
        }

                if (p < 0.28f) scale = Mathf.Lerp(HoverScale, 0.97f, p / 0.28f);
                else if (p < 0.62f) scale = Mathf.Lerp(0.97f, 1.04f, (p - 0.28f) / 0.34f);
                else scale = Mathf.Lerp(1.04f, 1f, (p - 0.62f) / 0.38f);
                var wobble = Mathf.Sin(p * Mathf.PI * 3f) * (1f - p) * 1.6f;
                transform.localScale = _baseScale * scale;
    90|                transform.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, wobble);
                if (_rt != null) _rt.anchoredPosition = _basePos;
                yield return null;
            }
            RestoreRest();
            _hover = 0f;
            _shake = 0f;
            _busy = false;
            if (then != null) then();
        }
   100|

        {
            transform.localScale = _baseScale;
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            if (_rt != null) _rt.anchoredPosition = _basePos;
        }
    }

### Turn 415 (Assistant)

主菜单和暂停菜单的按钮会改成悬停时明显变大，并把上面和下面的按钮推开。设置和确认框里的按钮仍只做轻微缩放。
[tool_request: Write]
- path: <USER_PROFILE>\Documents\GitHub\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\Assets\Scripts\Runtime\UI\MenuButtonColumn.cs
- contents: using System.Collections.Generic;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace EggRescue
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 竖排按钮：悬停的一颗放大，并把上下邻居推开。
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class MenuButtonColumn : MonoBehaviour
    {
        public const float HoverScale = 1.58f;
        const float HoverSpeed = 7f;
        const float ExtraGap = 14f;

        struct Slot
        {
            public MenuButtonMotion Motion;
            public RectTransform Rect;
            public Vector2 Rest;
            public Vector3 BaseScale;
            public float Height;
            public float Hover;
        }

        readonly List<Slot> _slots = new List<Slot>();

        public static void Bind(params Button[] buttons)
        {
            var list = new List<Button>();
            for (var i = 0; i < buttons.Length; i++)
            {
                if (buttons[i] != null) list.Add(buttons[i]);
            }
            if (list.Count == 0) return;
            var column = list[0].GetComponent<MenuButtonColumn>();
            if (column == null) column = list[0].gameObject.AddComponent<MenuButtonColumn>();
            column.SetMembers(list);
        }

        void SetMembers(List<Button> buttons)
        {
            _slots.Clear();
            for (var i = 0; i < buttons.Count; i++)
            {
                var button = buttons[i];
                var motion = button.GetComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
                if (motion == null) motion = button.gameObject.AddComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
                motion.SetColumn(this);
                var rt = button.transform as RectTransform;
                var height = rt != null ? rt.sizeDelta.y : 64f;
                if (height < 1f && rt != null) height = rt.rect.height;
                _slots.Add(new Slot
                {
                    Motion = motion,
                    Rect = rt,
                    Rest = rt != null ? rt.anchoredPosition : Vector2.zero,
                    BaseScale = button.transform.localScale,
                    Height = height > 1f ? height : 64f,
                    Hover = 0f
                });
            }
            _slots.Sort((a, b) => b.Rest.y.CompareTo(a.Rest.y));
        }

        void Update()
        {
            if (_slots.Count == 0) return;
            var dt = Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            var count = _slots.Count;
            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                var slot = _slots[i];
                var wants = slot.Motion != null && slot.Motion.WantsHover && slot.Motion.IsInteractable;
                var target = wants ? 1f : 0f;
                slot.Hover = Mathf.MoveTowards(slot.Hover, target, dt * HoverSpeed);
                _slots[i] = slot;
            }

            var shift = new float[count];
            for (var k = 0; k < count; k++)
            {
                var weight = _slots[k].Hover;
                if (weight <= 0.001f) continue;
                var push = _slots[k].Height * (HoverScale - 1f) * 0.5f * weight + ExtraGap * weight;
                for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
                {
                    if (i < k) shift[i] += push;
                    else if (i > k) shift[i] -= push;
                }
            }

            var front = 0;
            var frontHover = 0f;
            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                var slot = _slots[i];
                if (slot.Rect == null) continue;
                slot.Rect.anchoredPosition = slot.Rest + new Vector2(0f, shift[i]);
                if (!slot.Motion.Busy)
                {
                    var scale = Mathf.Lerp(1f, HoverScale, slot.Hover);
                    slot.Rect.localScale = slot.BaseScale * scale;
                    slot.Rect.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
                }
                if (slot.Hover > frontHover)
                {
                    frontHover = slot.Hover;
                    front = i;
                }
            }
            if (frontHover > 0.2f && _slots[front].Rect != null)
                RaiseAmongColumn(_slots[front].Rect);
        }

        void RaiseAmongColumn(RectTransform hovered)
        {
            var highest = hovered.GetSiblingIndex();
            for (var i = 0; i < _slots.Count; i++)
            {
                if (_slots[i].Rect == null) continue;
                var index = _slots[i].Rect.GetSiblingIndex();
                if (index > highest) highest = index;
            }
            hovered.SetSiblingIndex(highest);
        }
    }
}

[tool_request: Write]
- path: <USER_PROFILE>\Documents\GitHub\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\Assets\Scripts\Runtime\UI\MenuButtonMotion.cs
- contents: using System.Collections;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.Events;
using UnityEngine.EventSystems;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace EggRescue
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 按钮按下后先播一段缩放，再执行动作。竖排时的放大和推开由 MenuButtonColumn 负责。
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class MenuButtonMotion : MonoBehaviour, IPointerEnterHandler, IPointerExitHandler
    {
        const float SoloHoverScale = 1.04f;
        const float ClickDuration = 0.22f;

        Button _button;
        RectTransform _rt;
        Vector3 _baseScale;
        Vector2 _basePos;
        MenuButtonColumn _column;
        float _hover;
        bool _over;
        bool _busy;

        public bool WantsHover { get { return _over && !_busy; } }
        public bool Busy { get { return _busy; } }
        public bool IsInteractable { get { return _button == null || _button.interactable; } }

        void Awake()
        {
            _button = GetComponent<Button>();
            _rt = transform as RectTransform;
            _baseScale = transform.localScale;
            if (_rt != null) _basePos = _rt.anchoredPosition;
        }

        public void SetColumn(MenuButtonColumn column)
        {
            _column = column;
        }

        void OnDisable()
        {
            _busy = false;
            _over = false;
            _hover = 0f;
            if (_column != null) return;
            RestoreRest();
        }

        public void Play(UnityAction then)
        {
            if (_busy) return;
            if (_button != null && !_button.interactable) return;
            StartCoroutine(ClickRoutine(then));
        }

        public void OnPointerEnter(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            if (_button != null && !_button.interactable) return;
            _over = true;
        }

        public void OnPointerExit(PointerEventData eventData)
        {
            _over = false;
        }

        void Update()
        {
            if (_column != null || _busy || _rt == null) return;
            var target = _over && IsInteractable ? 1f : 0f;
            _hover = Mathf.MoveTowards(_hover, target, Time.unscaledDeltaTime * 8f);
            var blend = Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, _hover);
            transform.localScale = _baseScale * Mathf.Lerp(1f, SoloHoverScale, blend);
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
        }

        IEnumerator ClickRoutine(UnityAction then)
        {
            _busy = true;
            _over = false;
            var elapsed = 0f;
            var from = transform.localScale;
            while (elapsed < ClickDuration)
            {
                elapsed += Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
                var p = Mathf.Clamp01(elapsed / ClickDuration);
                var dip = p < 0.45f ? Mathf.Lerp(1f, 0.94f, p / 0.45f) : Mathf.Lerp(0.94f, 1f, (p - 0.45f) / 0.55f);
                transform.localScale = Vector3.Lerp(from, _baseScale, p) * (dip / Mathf.Max(0.01f, 1f));
                transform.localScale = Vector3.Lerp(from, _baseScale, Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, p));
                var punch = p < 0.4f ? Mathf.Lerp(1f, 0.92f, p / 0.4f) : Mathf.Lerp(0.92f, 1f, (p - 0.4f) / 0.6f);
                transform.localScale = Vector3.Lerp(from, _baseScale, Mathf.SmoothStep(0f, 1f, p)) * (p < 0.4f ? punch : 1f);
                yield return null;
            }
            transform.localScale = _baseScale;
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            if (_column == null && _rt != null) _rt.anchoredPosition = _basePos;
            _hover = 0f;
            _busy = false;
            if (then != null) then();
        }

        void RestoreRest()
        {
            transform.localScale = _baseScale;
            transform.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
            if (_rt != null) _rt.anchoredPosition = _basePos;
        }
    }
}

        public static void FitToLabel(Button button, bool alignLeft)

            var height = rt.sizeDelta.y;
            if (alignLeft)
            {
                var left = pos.x - rt.sizeDelta.x * rt.pivot.x;
                rt.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
                rt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(width, height);
                rt.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(left, pos.y);
            }
            else
            {
                rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, rt.pivot.y);
                rt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(width, height);
            }

### Turn 483 (Assistant)

运行时改位置和宽度会盖掉你在编辑器里的调整。我把按钮效果挂到场景和预制体上，休息时的样子直接摆在里面，悬停用一个预览滑条就能看。
[tool_request: Write]
- path: <USER_PROFILE>\Documents\GitHub\Mystery-At-Windmill-Farm\Assets\Scripts\Runtime\UI\MenuButtonColumn.cs
- contents: using System.Collections.Generic;
using TMPro;
using UnityEngine;
using UnityEngine.UI;

namespace EggRescue
{
    /// <summary>
    /// 竖排按钮的悬停放大、摇晃和推开。按钮列表和参数在 Inspector 里调。
    /// 编辑器里把 Preview 拖到 0 以上，可以不进播放直接看效果；调完请拖回 0 再保存。
    /// </summary>
    [ExecuteAlways]
    public sealed class MenuButtonColumn : MonoBehaviour
    {
        [SerializeField] Button[] buttons;
        [SerializeField] bool alignLeft = true;
        [SerializeField] float hoverScale = 1.72f;
        [SerializeField] float extraGap = 14f;
        [SerializeField] float wobbleDegrees = 1.6f;
        [SerializeField] float wobblePixels = 2.2f;
        [SerializeField] float hoverSpeed = 7f;
        [SerializeField] float labelPad = 28f;
        [SerializeField, Range(0f, 1f)] float preview;
        [SerializeField] int previewIndex;

        struct Slot
        {
            public MenuButtonMotion Motion;
            public RectTransform Rect;
            public Vector2 Rest;
            public Vector3 BaseScale;
            public float Height;
            public float Hover;
        }

        readonly List<Slot> _slots = new List<Slot>();
        bool _previewing;

        public bool AlignLeft { get { return alignLeft; } }

        void OnEnable()
        {
            if (preview > 0.001f) preview = 0f;
            Rebuild();
        }

        void OnDisable()
        {
            if (_previewing) Restore();
            _previewing = false;
        }

        void Update()
        {
            if (!Application.isPlaying)
            {
                EditPreview();
                return;
            }
            if (_slots.Count == 0) Rebuild();
            PlayUpdate();
        }

        void EditPreview()
        {
            if (preview > 0.001f)
            {
                if (!_previewing)
                {
                    Rebuild();
                    _previewing = true;
                }
                Apply(Mathf.Clamp(previewIndex, 0, Mathf.Max(0, _slots.Count - 1)), preview, true);
                return;
            }
            if (!_previewing) return;
            Restore();
            _previewing = false;
        }

        void PlayUpdate()
        {
            var dt = Time.unscaledDeltaTime;
            var count = _slots.Count;
            var hot = -1;
            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                var slot = _slots[i];
                var wants = slot.Motion != null && slot.Motion.WantsHover && slot.Motion.IsInteractable;
                if (wants) hot = i;
                slot.Hover = Mathf.MoveTowards(slot.Hover, wants ? 1f : 0f, dt * hoverSpeed);
                _slots[i] = slot;
            }
            Apply(hot, hot >= 0 ? _slots[hot].Hover : 0f, false);
        }

        void Apply(int hot, float hotWeight, bool previewPose)
        {
            var count = _slots.Count;
            if (count == 0) return;
            if (previewPose)
            {
                for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
                {
                    var slot = _slots[i];
                    slot.Hover = i == hot ? hotWeight : 0f;
                    _slots[i] = slot;
                }
            }

            var shift = new float[count];
            for (var k = 0; k < count; k++)
            {
                var weight = _slots[k].Hover;
                if (weight <= 0.001f) continue;
                var push = _slots[k].Height * (hoverScale - 1f) * 0.5f * weight + extraGap * weight;
                for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
                {
                    if (i < k) shift[i] += push;
                    else if (i > k) shift[i] -= push;
                }
            }

            var front = 0;
            var frontHover = 0f;
            for (var i = 0; i < count; i++)
            {
                var slot = _slots[i];
                if (slot.Rect == null) continue;
                var wave = Mathf.Sin(Time.unscaledTime * 5.5f + i) * slot.Hover;
                slot.Rect.anchoredPosition = slot.Rest + new Vector2(wave * wobblePixels, shift[i]);
                var busy = !previewPose && slot.Motion != null && slot.Motion.Busy;
                if (!busy)
                {
                    var scale = Mathf.Lerp(1f, hoverScale, slot.Hover);
                    slot.Rect.localScale = slot.BaseScale * scale;
                    slot.Rect.localRotation = Quaternion.Euler(0f, 0f, wave * wobbleDegrees);
                }
                if (slot.Hover > frontHover)
                {
                    frontHover = slot.Hover;
                    front = i;
                }
            }
            if (frontHover > 0.2f && _slots[front].Rect != null)
                RaiseAmongColumn(_slots[front].Rect);
        }

        void Restore()
        {
            for (var i = 0; i < _slots.Count; i++)
            {
                var slot = _slots[i];
                if (slot.Rect == null) continue;
                slot.Rect.anchoredPosition = slot.Rest;
                slot.Rect.localScale = slot.BaseScale;
                slot.Rect.localRotation = Quaternion.identity;
                slot.Hover = 0f;
                _slots[i] = slot;
            }
        }

        void Rebuild()
        {
            _slots.Clear();
            if (buttons == null) return;
            for (var i = 0; i < buttons.Length; i++)
            {
                var button = buttons[i];
                if (button == null) continue;
                var motion = button.GetComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
                if (motion == null) motion = button.gameObject.AddComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
                motion.SetColumn(this);
                var rt = button.transform as RectTransform;
                var height = rt != null ? rt.sizeDelta.y : 64f;
                if (height < 1f && rt != null) height = rt.rect.height;
                _slots.Add(new Slot
                {
                    Motion = motion,
                    Rect = rt,
                    Rest = rt != null ? rt.anchoredPosition : Vector2.zero,
                    BaseScale = button.transform.localScale,
                    Height = height > 1f ? height : 64f
                });
            }
            _slots.Sort((a, b) => b.Rest.y.CompareTo(a.Rest.y));
        }

        void RaiseAmongColumn(RectTransform hovered)
        {
            var highest = hovered.GetSiblingIndex();
            for (var i = 0; i < _slots.Count; i++)
            {
                if (_slots[i].Rect == null) continue;
                var index = _slots[i].Rect.GetSiblingIndex();
                if (index > highest) highest = index;
            }
            hovered.SetSiblingIndex(highest);
        }

        [ContextMenu("按文字收紧背景")]
        public void FitWidths()
        {
            if (preview > 0.001f) return;
            if (buttons == null) return;
            for (var i = 0; i < buttons.Length; i++)
                FitToLabel(buttons[i], alignLeft, labelPad);
            Rebuild();
        }

        public static void FitToLabel(Button button, bool alignLeft, float pad)
        {
            if (button == null) return;
            var rt = button.transform as RectTransform;
            var label = button.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>();
            if (rt == null || label == null) return;
            label.enableWordWrapping = false;
            label.alignment = alignLeft ? TextAlignmentOptions.MidlineLeft : TextAlignmentOptions.Center;
            var labelRt = label.rectTransform;
            labelRt.offsetMin = new Vector2(pad, labelRt.offsetMin.y);
            labelRt.offsetMax = new Vector2(-pad, labelRt.offsetMax.y);
            label.ForceMeshUpdate();
            var textWidth = label.GetPreferredValues(label.text).x;
            if (textWidth < 8f) return;
            var width = Mathf.Ceil(textWidth) + pad * 2f;
            var pos = rt.anchoredPosition;
            var height = rt.sizeDelta.y;
            if (alignLeft)
            {
                var left = pos.x - rt.sizeDelta.x * rt.pivot.x;
                rt.pivot = new Vector2(0f, 0.5f);
                rt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(width, height);
                rt.anchoredPosition = new Vector2(left, pos.y);
            }
            else
            {
                rt.pivot = new Vector2(0.5f, rt.pivot.y);
                rt.sizeDelta = new Vector2(width, height);
            }
        }
    }
}

        void HookPrefabButton(Transform root, string name, string key, TMP_FontAsset font, UnityAction action)
        {
            var child = root.Find(name);
            if (child == null) return;
            var button = child.GetComponent<Button>();
            var label = child.GetComponentInChildren<TMP_Text>();
            if (label != null)

            var motion = button.GetComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
            if (motion == null) motion = button.gameObject.AddComponent<MenuButtonMotion>();
            button.onClick.AddListener(() => motion.Play(action));
        }
